Here, we present a comparative analysis of generative models for transcriptomic data, investigating strategies to incorporate prior biological knowledge via gene graphs. This ensures that synthetic data capture real-world gene patterns, maintaining their usefulness for downstream tasks. In particular, we introduce and benchmark three variants of the Generative Adversarial Network. Among the alternatives, MK-TGAN - an innovative multi-kernel, Graph Neural Network-based model - stands out for its performance in terms of both the realism and utility of the generated data. Unlike other methods, MK-TGAN leverages prior knowledge graphs by exploiting graph neural networks. Our results show that prior knowledge integration strategies improve performance, and that MK-TGAN consistently produces synthetic samples with superior realism and biological plausibility.

</details>


### [231] [Virtual Temperature Sensors in Power Transformers Using Neural Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13260)
*Berk Hadzhamolla,Alexander Johannes Stasik,Signe Riemer-Sørensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理感知的神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）框架，用于从真实世界时间序列数据预测电力变压器热行为。该框架将简化的传热方程直接集成到Neural ODE公式中，并在挪威不同地区的15台不同设计和冷却机制的变压器数据集上进行了评估，结果表明该方法是标准化、物理感知且鲁棒的预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变压器热建模方法存在明显不足：数值方法（FEM/CFD）精度高但计算昂贵且需要复杂网格生成，难以实时或大规模应用；集总参数模型实用但依赖于特定变压器的热常数，无法捕捉动态响应；纯数据驱动的ML方法需要大量高质量训练数据，可能产生物理不一致或不可解释的结果。因此需要一个兼具物理合理性和数据驱动灵活性的标准化方法。

Method: 开发了一个物理感知的Neural ODE框架：利用Neural ODE在连续时间中建模系统动力学，提供平滑轨迹预测并自然表征连续演化的热动力学；关键贡献是将简化热传导方程直接整合进Neural ODE的公式构造中，使模型同时具备物理先验和数据适应性。

Result: 在来自挪威不同地区的15台变压器（涵盖不同设计和冷却机制）的数据集上进行了评估，结果表明所提出的Neural ODE框架为异构变压器单元提供了标准化、物理感知且鲁棒的预测方法。

Conclusion: 物理感知的Neural ODE框架为跨异构变压器单元的温度预测提供了一种标准化且可靠的方法，弥合了物理模型与数据驱动方法之间的鸿沟，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Accurate modeling and forecasting of power transformer thermal behavior are critical for reliability, asset lifetime, and optimized power system operation. Numerical approaches such as finite element methods (FEM) and computational fluid dynamics (CFD) offer high fidelity but are computationally expensive, require complex mesh generation, and are often impractical for real-time or large-scale applications, particularly when transformer geometries are unknown. Lumped-parameter thermal models are more practical but depend on transformer-specific thermal constants and may fail to capture dynamic responses under varying operating and environmental conditions. Purely data-driven machine learning methods, including artificial neural networks, convolutional neural networks, and long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, have shown success in forecasting transformer temperatures but typically require large volumes of high-quality training data and may produce physically inconsistent or uninterpretable results. This paper develops a physics-aware Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (Neural ODE) framework for forecasting transformer thermal behavior from real-world time-series data. Neural ODEs model system dynamics in continuous time, providing smooth trajectory prediction and a natural representation of continuously evolving thermal dynamics. A key contribution is the integration of simplified heat-transfer equations directly into the Neural ODE formulation. The model is evaluated across datasets from fifteen transformers in different regions of Norway with varying designs and cooling mechanisms. The results demonstrate that the developed Neural ODE framework provides a standardized, physics-aware, and robust forecasting approach for heterogeneous transformer units.

</details>


### [232] [Into the ORBIT for Time Series: Training Regimes for Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13262)
*Hongjie Xia,Yiding Liu,Yifan Hu,Peiyuan Liu,Zewei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ORBIT训练范式，通过显式控制预训练分布（数据暴露、样本/目标变量/上下文窗口/预测长度的采样，以及单训练阶段内不同上下文长度和预测长度的增量训练）来训练Falcon-2.0——一个支持缺失值感知的三通道patch tokenization和并行patch预测的简单单变量encoder-only Transformer。此外引入Rank-Guided Cross-Depth Alignment训练目标，利用浅层向深层对齐（stop-gradient教师）以提升性能且不增加推理成本。在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench上展现出跨多种领域与频率的强零样本预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）的进步主要依赖架构创新，而针对大规模异构语料库的训练机制探索不足，导致预训练分布在领域不平衡、上下文需求、预测长度和缺失性方面缺乏精细控制。作者旨在使预训练分布显式化和可控，以提升模型在下游零样本预测任务上的泛化能力。

Method: ORBIT包含两大组件：(1) Bootstrap Multi-Level Sampling——控制数据集暴露程度并采样记录、目标变量、上下文窗口和预测长度；(2) Omni-Range Incremental Training——在同一训练阶段内动态变化上下文长度和预测长度。在此框架下训练Falcon-2.0，采用缺失值感知的三通道patch tokenization与并行patch预测，并引入Rank-Guided Cross-Depth Alignment目标（浅层以深层表示作为stop-gradient教师进行对齐），不增加推理成本。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench基准上，Falcon-2.0在多样领域与频率的零样本预测任务中表现出强劲性能，验证了ORBIT训练范式与所提出训练目标的有效性。

Conclusion: ORBIT证明了对时间序列预训练分布进行显式控制与细粒度采样调度，是提升基础模型零样本泛化能力的关键因素。同时，跨深度表示对齐作为一种无需额外推理开销的训练目标，可以替代复杂的架构创新来提升模型效果。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have advanced primarily through architectural innovation, while training regimes for large-scale heterogeneous corpora remain under-explored. As a result, pre-training distributions are often poorly controlled with respect to domain imbalance, context requirements, prediction horizons, and missingness. We introduce ORBIT (Omni-Range Bootstrap Incremental Training), a training paradigm that makes this distribution explicit and controllable. ORBIT combines Bootstrap Multi-Level Sampling, which controls dataset exposure and samples records, target variables, context windows, and prediction horizons, with Omni-Range Incremental Training, which varies context lengths and prediction horizons throughout a single training stage. Under ORBIT, we train Falcon-2.0, a simple univariate encoder-only Transformer with missingness-aware triple-channel patch tokenization and parallel patch prediction. We further introduce Rank-Guided Cross-Depth Alignment, a training objective that uses late-layer representations as stop-gradient teachers for shallow layers without additional inference cost. Evaluations on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench demonstrate strong zero-shot forecasting performance across diverse domains and frequencies.

</details>


### [233] [EEG Decoding Using CNN and LSTM Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13285)
*Athanasios Karagounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合CNN与双向LSTM的混合深度学习架构，用于运动想象脑电（MI-EEG）信号的可靠解码。CNN从原始EEG中学习高层次时空表征，bi-LSTM建模特征间的时间依赖关系，在公开数据集和私有数据集上均展现出稳健的两分类和三分类性能，并表现出良好的跨被试（subject-independent）解码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动想象脑机接口对脑卒中或神经退行性疾病患者具有重要意义，但MI-EEG信号含有大量噪声且与脑活动之间存在复杂且弱相关的关系，导致可靠解码具有挑战性。尽管深度学习能够直接从EEG信号中学习表征，但其在MI-EEG特征学习中的应用仍相对有限，亟需更有效的特征学习架构。

Method: 提出一种混合深度学习架构，将卷积神经网络（CNN）与双向长短期记忆网络（bi-LSTM）相结合。CNN负责从原始MI-EEG记录中学习高层次的空间和时间表征，bi-LSTM则对提取的特征之间的时间依赖关系和关联进行建模。模型在公开数据集和私有EEG采集系统数据集上进行了评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，CNN&bi-LSTM架构在二分类和三分类运动想象任务上均表现出稳健的性能，并在所评估的方法中展现出有前景的跨被试（subject-independent）解码能力。

Conclusion: CNN与bi-LSTM的混合架构能够有效学习MI-EEG的时空表征和时序依赖关系，为运动想象BCI提供了可靠的解码方案，具备跨被试泛化能力，具有一定的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Motor imagery (MI) brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) have emerged as a promising approach for establishing flexible communication pathways between the human brain and external devices , particularly for individuals affected by stroke or neurodegenerative disorders. Reliable decoding of motor-imagery electroencephalography (MI-EEG) remains challenging because EEG recordings contain substantial noise and exhibit complex, weakly informative relationships with the underlying brain activity. Although deep learning provides an effective means of learning representations directly from EEG signals, its application to MI-EEG feature learning remains comparatively limited. This study introduces a hybrid deep-learning architecture that integrates a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a bidirectional long short-term memory (bi-LSTM) network. The CNN is used to learn high-level spatial and temporal representations directly from raw MI-EEG recordings, whereas the bi-LSTM models temporal dependencies and relationships among the extracted features. The proposed approach is evaluated using both a publicly available dataset and a privately acquired dataset obtained with an EEG acquisition system. The experimental results indicate that the CNN\&bi-LSTM architecture provides robust performance for both two- and three-class motor-imagery classification and demonstrates promising subject-independent decoding capability across the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [234] [Large-scale Testing Global Optimization Methods with Black-box Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13296)
*Wojciech Zarzecki,Jarosław Arabas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将黑盒对抗攻击（BBAA）问题作为高维全局优化基准测试套件，并验证了多种进化算法和元启发式算法在该类问题上的有效性，从而推动全局优化方法与现代机器学习需求的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局优化基准测试套件规模适中，但基于少数可追溯到1970年代的解析函数，存在偏置全局优化方法发展的风险。

Method: 论证黑盒对抗攻击（BBAA）任务可作为高维亚空间中的全局优化基准，并通过实验评估多种进化算法及其他元启发式算法在示例BBAA问题上的求解效率。

Result: 多种进化算法和元启发式算法在解决示例BBAA问题中表现出有效性，证明了BBAA作为全局优化基准的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作促使全局优化方法向现代机器学习领域的挑战与需求靠拢，推动两者融合。

Abstract: Existing global optimization benchmark suites are of a moderate size and are based on a small number of analytical functions that date back even to the 1970s. This causes a risk of biasing the development of global optimization methods. We argue that the tasks related to the black-box adversarial attack (BBAA) can serve as valuable global optimization benchmark in many-dimensional space. We demonstrate the efficiency of several types of evolutionary algorithms and other metaheuristics in solving example BBAA problems. Thus, we take a step towards convergence of global optimization methods to the challenges and needs that arise in the modern machine learning field.

</details>


### [235] [The Time Value of Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13297)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lineage-Value Policy Gradients（LVPG），一个面向自动化交易策略发现的长时域Actor-Critic框架，将进化搜索中的"弱子代可能成为有价值祖先"这一延迟回报机制形式化为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程中的"进化时间价值"，并通过专门的策略头解耦搜索控制，实现在相同资源约束下显著优于即时回报优化的策略发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在进化搜索中，即时回报控制方式存在根本性缺陷：它只依据突变后子代的即时表现进行惩罚或奖励，却忽略了弱子代可能作为有价值的祖先，使后续高适应度区域变得可达的延迟效用。这种"时间价值"被现有方法所无视，导致在有限预算下无法充分挖掘进化的长期潜力。

Method: LVPG将进化搜索的延迟回报动态形式化为有限时域MDP。架构上，在共享生成骨干上解耦出两个专门策略头：(1) 基于自举法的critic头，从多步突变树中估计有限时域谱系潜力价值；(2) actor头，根据剩余搜索预算动态调节突变强度。通过这样的长时域信用分配机制，实现路径敏感的非单调搜索。

Result: 在90组配对实验（匹配操作符、谱系监督、折叠、种子和预算）中，路径基信用分配显著加速了有限预算搜索，将验证集best-so-far AUC提升0.394个Sharpe单位。LVPG产生的临时回退比即时回报优化更少，且从回退中恢复的频率更高。

Conclusion: 有限时域谱系价值能够带来更具选择性的非单调搜索，在完全相同的资源约束下产生更强的交易策略。长时域信用分配是进化搜索中不可忽视的关键机制，LVPG为自动化策略发现提供了更优的范式。

Abstract: In evolutionary search, a weak child can be a valuable ancestor that makes high-fitness regions reachable. Immediate-return control is blind to this delayed utility, penalizing mutations through their immediate offspring even when they open productive future lineages. We formalize this hidden dynamic as the time value of evolution within a finite-horizon Markov decision process. To exploit it, we introduce Lineage-Value Policy Gradients (LVPG), a long-horizon actor-critic framework for automated trading policy discovery. Our architecture decouples search control into specialized policy heads over a shared generative backbone: a bootstrapped critic head estimates the value of finite-horizon lineage potential from multi-step mutation trees, while an actor head dynamically modulates mutation intensity over the remaining search budget. We isolate the impact of long-horizon credit assignment against immediate-return optimization across 90 paired runs under matched operators, lineage supervision, folds, seeds, and budgets. Path-based credit assignment substantially accelerates finite-budget search, increasing validation best-so-far AUC by 0.394 Sharpe units. LVPG also produces fewer temporary regressions than immediate-return optimization and recovers from them more often. Finite-horizon lineage value yields more selective non-monotonic search and stronger policies within identical resource constraints.

</details>


### [236] [A Probe Direction Is a Property of Its Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13329)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"评估感知探针"（evaluation-awareness probes）测量中的一个根本缺陷：该类方法的关键自由参数——"宣布评估的提示词"如何选择——未被报告，而该选择本身就能改变得分及其随模型规模变化的趋势方向。作者证明之前两项结论相反的研究可仅靠提示词选择在同一设计中复现，模型本身只解释了报告分数中很小一部分方差，而大部分方差来自模型与提示词的交互。此外，这类探针所依赖的划分在很大程度上可由表层形式（surface form）单独分离，即使完全不携带评估信息的探针方向也能复现已发表分数的大部分。作者因此得出结论：单提示词设计无法支持模型间比较，并给出了具有说服力的比较所需的提示词数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型在感知到被测试时可能出现不同行为，这会破坏评估的可信度。已有工作通过从模型激活中读出这种"感知"来度量它。然而，用于此目的的探针仪器存在一个未被披露的自由参数——提示词的选择——这可能导致测量结果不可靠、甚至趋势方向相反，因此有必要检验该测量方法的有效性和跨模型可比性。

Method: 作者固定任务文本，系统性地变化"宣布评估"的不同提示词表述，考察探针得分随提示词选择而变化的情况。通过方差分解分析模型、提示词及其交互对报告分数方差的贡献；同时构造仅依赖表层形式（不携带评估语义信息）的探针方向作为对照，检验已有已发表分数的可复现性。

Result: 结果表明：探针得分乃至其随模型规模变化的趋势方向由提示词选择而非模型本身决定；两篇关于趋势符号有分歧的已发表研究可由同一设计仅凭提示词选择而复现；模型本身只解释了报告分数方差中很小一部分，大部分方差来自模型-提示词的交互项，因此增加评估样本数量无法修复测量，而变换提示词可以；仅靠表层形式分离的探针方向也能复现已发表分数的大部分。

Conclusion: 单提示词的探针设计无法支持模型间的比较，需要将提示词视为测量设计的一个刻面（facet）而非实现细节。作者给出了进行有说服力的模型间比较所需的提示词数量建议。

Abstract: A model that behaves differently when it senses it is being tested would undermine the evaluations we rely on, so recent work has sought to read that sense directly from a model's activations. The standard instrument contrasts activations on prompts that announce an evaluation against prompts that do not, and reports how well the resulting direction separates held-out cases. That number is then compared across models and correlated with scale. We observe that the instrument has a free parameter its readings do not disclose: "a prompt that announces an evaluation" is not a prompt but a choice among many, and nothing in the method fixes which. Holding the task text fixed and varying only that choice, we find that the reported score, and even the direction in which it trends with model size, follows the prompt rather than the model; two published studies that disagree about the sign of that trend are both reproducible from a single design, by choice of prompt alone. Treating the prompt as a facet of a measurement design rather than an implementation detail, we find the model under study accounts for a small share of the variance in the number reported about it, and most of the rest lies in how each model responds to each prompt: collecting more evaluation items cannot repair the measurement, while varying prompts can. A further check finds that the split these probes are scored on is largely separable from surface form alone, so a direction carrying no information about evaluation at all still reproduces a substantial fraction of each published score. We conclude that a single-prompt design cannot support comparison between models, and we give the number of prompts a defensible comparison requires.

</details>


### [237] [Training AI Scientists to Replicate Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13331)
*Damon Falck,Samer Sabri,Anja Surina,Thom Foster,Anya Sims,Sam Devlin,Dylan Rogers,Tantum Collins,Kaloyan Aleksiev,Louis Kirsch,Edward Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Replica，一个用于论文复现的可扩展任务空间，并引入基于自动生成评分标准的低噪声评判器来评估复现质量。作者后训练了一个270亿参数的"AI科学家"智能体Faraday，利用编码智能体作为工具，在留出复现任务上超越了Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.5。定性分析显示Faraday采用了更符合科学原则的方法，为无需复杂框架的长时程科学创新AI智能体奠定了基石。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文复现是科学知识可靠性的基石，但复现过程往往涉及先前未充分明确的细节，需要类似开放式研究的假设驱动探索。目前缺乏可扩展、有可靠奖励信号的论文复现任务空间，以及能够在复现任务上表现出色的AI智能体。

Method: 1) 构建Replica，一个可扩展的论文复现任务空间；2) 引入自动生成的rubric评判器，噪声低且与人类复现质量评估高度一致，作为奖励信号来源；3) 后训练一个27B参数的"AI Scientist"智能体Faraday，将编码智能体作为工具集成到其工作流程中。

Result: Faraday在留出的复现任务上超越了Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.5。定性分析表明，Faraday采用了更科学、更有原则的探索方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建能够进行长时程科学创新而不依赖复杂框架的AI智能体提供了重要基础，展示了预训练后微调方法在科学智能体领域的潜力。

Abstract: The replicability of papers is a cornerstone of scientific knowledge, ensuring the reliability of existing results and providing a base for further experiments. The act of replication typically illuminates details that were previously underspecified, and thus requires similar hypothesis-driven exploration to open-ended research. In this work, we develop Replica, a scalable task space for paper replication. To provide reward signal, we introduce an auto-generated rubric-based judge that has low noise and agrees with human assessment of replication quality. We post-train Faraday, a 27B-parameter "AI Scientist" agent that leverages coding agents as tools, surpassing the performance of Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-5.5 on held-out replication tasks. Qualitative analysis of individual rollouts reveals that Faraday adopts a more scientifically-principled approach. We believe that our results provide a stepping stone towards AI agents capable of long-horizon scientific innovation without requiring complex harnesses.

</details>


### [238] [Neural Quadratic Forms: A Unified Minimal Model for Sudden Learning and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13335)
*Liu Ziyin,Yizhou Xu,Tomaso Poggio,Isaac Chuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出，即使采用梯度下降训练平滑代价函数的神经网络，学习过程也会呈现阶梯式特征（长平台期后突然下降），而训练损失却遵循平滑幂律。作者证明对称性（单元重标号不变性）决定了相关的集体变量，并推导出一个普适的领头阶展开式 \\(\Tr[WW^{\top}A(x)]\\)，其中所有架构细节都体现在一个"结构矩阵" A(x) 中。以此将感知机、注意力层、混合专家和卷积统一为同一模型的不同 A。训练动力学在序参量 M=WW^{\top} 上闭合，并在共享特征基条件下约化为 Lotka-Volterra 方程，其模式逐个开启。平台期是平滑流动的奇异极限，未分辨的众多模式合并为可预测指数的幂律。理论与数值实验在多种训练方法和架构上均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络训练中出现的两种看似矛盾的行为——代价函数呈现阶梯式平台期，而训练损失呈平滑幂律——并解释为何结构差异巨大的架构表现出相似行为。作者认为这种共性暗示存在少数相关的集体变量，目标是通过对称性识别这些变量并建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 利用网络层关于单元重标号的不变性对称性，结合平滑性和单元梯度在原点消失的条件，推导出关于近零权重展开的普适领头阶形式 \\(\Tr[WW^{\top}A(x)]\\)。对每类架构（感知机、注意力层、混合专家、卷积）显式计算结构矩阵 A(x)，从而将其统一到一个模型中。训练动力学在序参量 M=WW^{\top} 上闭合；当数据矩阵共享特征基时，约化为 Lotka-Volterra 方程，模式按时间先后依次开启。分析平台期作为平滑流动奇异极限的行为，并推导未分辨多模式合并为幂律时的指数。

Result: 作者推导出普适的领头阶展开式，将所有架构统一到单一模型，不同架构仅体现在结构矩阵 A(x) 的差异上。训练动力学在序参量上闭合，并约化为 Lotka-Volterra 方程，模式逐个开启。初始权重越小，模式开启时间间隔越大；平台期对应于平滑流动的奇异极限，而多模式未分辨时则合并为训练时间中的幂律，其指数可由理论预测。数值实验在多种训练方法和架构下均验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 神经网络的阶梯式学习与平滑幂律损失是同一普适动力学的两面：对称性强制了近零权重展开的普适领头阶形式，将所有架构统一到以结构矩阵 A(x) 区分的一个模型。平台期是平滑流动的奇异极限，而幂律是多模式合并的宏观表现。这为理解不同架构训练的共性本质提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: Neural networks trained by gradient descent on a smooth cost function can nevertheless learn in steps: the cost holds on long plateaus and then drops abruptly. Meanwhile, training losses instead follow smooth power laws. Variants of both behaviors occur in architectures with very different microscopic structures, which is the signature of a few relevant collective variables. We show that a symmetry fixes what those variables are: a network layer is a sum over interchangeable units, so relabeling the units leaves it unchanged; given smoothness and the condition that a unit's gradient vanish at the origin, symmetry then enforces a universal leading form for the expansion about the near-zero weights present at the start of training, the quadratic $\Tr[WW^{\top}A(x)]$, in which every architectural detail is confined to a single ``structure matrix" $A(x)$ that we compute for each architecture. Perceptrons, attention layers, mixtures of experts, and convolutions become one model at different $A$. Its training dynamics then close on the ``order parameter" $M=WW^{\top}$ and, whenever the data matrices share an eigenbasis, reduce to a Lotka--Volterra equation whose modes switch on one after another. The smaller the initial weights, the further apart the switch-on times, and the plateaus appear as a singular limit of a smooth flow; when many modes are unresolved the same events merge into a power law in training time whose exponent the theory predicts. We confirm both numerically across training methods and architectures.

</details>


### [239] [Where You Measure Decides What You Measure: Position Selection in Ablation-Based SAE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13337)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器中，衡量潜在特征因果效应时默认在激活最强处测量，但该位置由字典而非实验者决定。不同字典对同一潜在特征会选择不同测量token，导致比较失真。作者训练六个仅拟合选择不同、潜在语义一致的字典，发现大多数"字典分歧"实际是测量位置差异（方差贡献从7.6%/11.9%降至近零）。更多数据反而加剧问题。作者提出一行代码即可修正的评估协议，并审计了五篇已发表论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器的标准因果评估协议存在隐含缺陷：测量潜在特征效应时选择的token位置取决于字典本身而非实验者意图，且这一选择几乎从不被报告。当比较不同字典时，实际上可能在不同位置进行比较，导致对"字典是否同意该潜在特征"的判断失真。

Method: 1) 取Google发布的同一模型上的两个SAE，通过解码器相似性匹配潜在特征，检查它们选择测量位置的差异。2) 从同一初始条件训练六个仅拟合选择不同的自编码器，保证潜在特征语义一致，量化位置选择对"字典分歧"方差的贡献。3) 在16倍范围内变化语料规模，测试更多数据是否缓解该问题。4) 提出修正协议（在相同token处测量），并审计五篇已发表论文。

Result: 即使两个字典对潜在特征的编码几乎相同，它们仍会在大部分匹配对上选择不同测量token。位置效应解释了比较中7.6%和11.9%的方差，在统一测量位置后降至接近零。增大语料规模不缓解反而加剧了位置分歧。修正只需一行评估代码，审计显示已发表论文普遍未报告该位置。

Conclusion: 因果数字若不报告其测量位置，描述的是该token本身和该潜在特征一样多。稀疏自编码器的因果评估必须包含测量位置的协议，否则跨字典比较不可靠。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are meant to name the things a language model computes, and the usual way to check that a latent matters is to switch it off and see what changes. But a latent fires at many tokens, and the effect has to be measured at one of them. The convention is to measure where the latent fires hardest. That choice is almost never reported, and it is not made by the experimenter: it is made by the dictionary under evaluation. Change the dictionary and the measurement moves to a different token. We show this is not a detail. Take two sparse autoencoders released by Google for the same model and match their latents by decoder similarity: even among the pairs the two dictionaries encode almost identically, they pick different tokens for a large share of them. Two dictionaries compared under the usual protocol are therefore very often compared at different places. To separate the convention from the dictionaries we train six autoencoders from one initialisation, differing only in fitting choices, so that a latent means the same thing in each. Most of the variance such a comparison reads as "these dictionaries disagree about this latent" turns out to be the position instead: it falls from 7.6% and 11.9% of variance to near zero once every dictionary is measured at the same token. More evaluation data does not rescue it. Across a sixteenfold range of corpus sizes the dictionaries agree less about where to measure, not more, so the problem grows with scale. The correction is one line of evaluation code. We give the protocol an ablation-based causal number must report to be comparable across papers, and an audit of five published papers against it. In short: a causal number reported without its position describes the token it was taken at as much as the latent it was taken from.

</details>


### [240] [Simulation-to-real transfer learning for infrared spectroscopic chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13341)
*Yusen Tan,Yixuan Chen,Zheng Fang,Pan Liu,Yifan Li,Qinyu Guo,Zhedong Lin,Yuqiang Li,Xiangxiang Zeng,Tong Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了UltraIR，一个拥有超过1亿参数的红外光谱基础模型，通过约6000万张模拟红外光谱的预训练（涵盖光谱重建、分子指纹相似性对齐和官能团预测），实现了从模拟到真实世界的迁移学习，在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物鉴定与定量、细菌分类、药用植物产地溯源、微塑料分类和土壤性质预测等多项任务中均优于传统机器学习和任务特定深度学习基线，并在有限标注数据和零样本跨仪器推理场景下表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统红外光谱解析依赖人工且难以规模化，而现有机器学习方法多为任务或数据集定制，需要大量标注数据，跨任务和跨实验数据集的迁移能力差。作者旨在开发一个通用、可迁移的IR光谱基础模型，以支持从分子到复杂样品的化学传感与分析。

Method: UltraIR采用基础模型范式：在约6000万个模拟红外光谱上进行预训练，预训练任务包括光谱重建（spectral reconstruction）、分子指纹相似性对齐（molecular fingerprint similarity alignment）和官能团预测（functional-group prediction），随后通过任务特定标签或目标进行下游适配，实现模拟到真实的迁移学习。

Result: UltraIR在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物组分鉴定与定量、细菌分类、药用植物产地溯源与成分定量、微塑料分类以及土壤性质预测等多样化下游任务中，均超越了传统机器学习和任务特定的深度学习基线。该模型在有限标注实验光谱及跨FTIR仪器与实验室的零样本推断中表现强劲。

Conclusion: UltraIR为红外光谱化学感知提供了一条高适应性和数据高效的路径，证明了基于大规模模拟数据预训练的基础模型能够有效泛化到复杂真实世界样品的多类分析任务，有望替代劳动密集型的传统光谱解析方法。

Abstract: Infrared (IR) spectroscopy is widely used for chemical sensing, but extracting reliable chemical information from spectra remains challenging. Conventional interpretation is labor-intensive, relies on prior knowledge and reference spectra, and is difficult to scale, whereas most machine-learning methods are tailored to individual tasks or datasets, require large labeled training sets, and transfer poorly across analytical objectives and experimental datasets. Here we introduce UltraIR, a foundation model for IR spectroscopy with more than 100 million parameters that enables simulation-to-real transfer learning for chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples. UltraIR is pretrained on approximately 60 million simulated IR spectra using spectral reconstruction, molecular fingerprint similarity alignment, and functional-group prediction, then adapted to downstream objectives with task-specific labels or targets. Across functional-group prediction, molecular structure elucidation, physicochemical property prediction, mixture-component identification and quantification, bacterial classification, medicinal-herb geographic origin traceability and constituent quantification, microplastics classification, and soil property prediction, UltraIR outperforms conventional machine-learning and task-specific deep-learning baselines. It performs strongly with limited labeled experimental spectra and in zero-shot inference for the same analytical task across Fourier-transform infrared spectrometers and laboratories, providing a route to adaptable, data-efficient chemical sensing from complex real-world samples.

</details>


### [241] [When Local Variance Optimality Is Not Enough: RoPE-Aligned Q/K Rotations for Dynamic 4-Bit Quantisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13365)
*Shuhan Wang,Yilin Luo,Nan Xu,Chi Wang Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于旋转的后训练量化中，与RoPE交换的逐对（pairwise）正交变换是否优于全头混合变换。结果表明，在动态W4A4KV4设置下，头共享的逐对旋转配置无法改善精度，且不像全头Hadamard变换那样能满足困惑度约束；通过从两通道到全头混合的受控插值实验，发现K范围、相对量化误差和困惑度退化随混合支持范围增加而减小，说明结构化代理目标的最优性不一定降低量化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE将注意力头划分为二维频率对，现有工作已建立与RoPE可交换的逐对旋转，但尚不清楚这种尊重RoPE分解的变换能否在量化中优于全头混合。作者希望探究这一结构性问题。

Method: 作者证明了在频率不同时，不存在其他单头正交映射与RoPE交换（反向结果）；推导了在池化协方差、位置平均的代理目标下最小化较大通道方差的旋转角度，并验证实现达到解析最小值；通过受控插值从两通道混合到全头混合，分析K范围、相对量化误差和困惑度退化的变化。

Result: 头共享的逐对配置在动态W4A4KV4设置下未改善精度，且在四个检查点上均比全头Hadamard困惑度更高；逐对旋转与Hadamard组合能满足±0.05-PPL区间准则；从K单独估计共享角可改善逐对配置但无法弥合与全头混合的差距；受控插值显示随着混合支持范围增大，K范围、相对量化误差和困惑度退化均减小。

Conclusion: 结构化代理目标的最优性并不保证降低实际量化误差，原因是代理目标控制的是位置平均的池化协方差二阶矩，而动态量化器根据逐token的组范围设定步长，且逐对变换仅有两通道混合支持，与量化器的缩放统计不一致。

Abstract: Rotation-based post-training quantisation commonly applies an orthogonal transform across an entire attention head to reduce outlier-induced error. RoPE instead partitions each head into two-dimensional frequency pairs, raising the question of whether a transform respecting this decomposition can improve on full-head mixing. Prior work has established the per-pair rotations that commute with RoPE. We state the converse result that, for distinct frequencies, no other single-head orthogonal map commutes with RoPE. For the head-shared parameterisation used in our experiments, we then derive the rotation angle that minimises the larger channel variance under a pooled-covariance, position-averaged surrogate and verify that the implementation attains its analytic minimum. The evaluated head-shared pairwise configuration does not improve accuracy in the tested dynamic W4A4KV4 setting. Across four checkpoints, replacing the full-head Hadamard with this configuration increases perplexity at both short and long context lengths. Composing the pairwise rotation with the Hadamard satisfies the selected $\pm0.05$-PPL interval criterion under the default estimator. Estimating the shared angle from K alone improves pairwise-only on every checkpoint but does not close its gap to full-head mixing. The analytic objective controls a position-averaged second moment of a pooled calibration covariance, whereas the dynamic quantiser sets its step from a tokenwise group range. The pairwise transform also has only two-channel mixing support. Along a controlled interpolation from two-channel to full-head mixing, K range, relative quantisation error, and perplexity degradation decrease as support increases. These results show that optimality for a structured surrogate need not reduce quantisation error when the surrogate and mixing support are misaligned with the quantiser's scale-setting statistic.

</details>


### [242] [Reduced Matrix Multiplication: Input-Adaptive Matrix-Product Reduction for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13426)
*Zixuan Lan,Yanhong Li,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、输入自适应的推理加速方法RMM（Reduced Matrix Multiplication），通过沿收缩维度选择信息丰富的切片来缩减Transformer矩阵乘法，在不修改模型权重的前提下实现精度与效率的可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型性能优异但推理成本高昂，主要源于重复的高维矩阵乘法运算。现有多数加速方法需要重新训练或修改模型结构，缺乏一种无需训练、可适配不同输入的高效推理优化方案。

Method: RMM方法在推理时沿矩阵乘法的收缩维度选择具有信息量的切片进行缩减计算，通过简单的保留比例（retention-ratio）控制实现平滑且可预测的精度-效率权衡，无需修改模型权重。研究在1B到70B参数的多规模语言模型上进行系统评估，并扩展到多模态视觉-语言推理。

Result: 实验发现：缩减容忍度依赖于模型家族、任务类型、组件和保留比例，且通常随模型规模增大而改善；在中等缩减比例下，RMM在判别式任务、自回归生成和长上下文场景中均保持稳健；机制性消融揭示了Transformer内部的结构性不对称——注意力侧的矩阵乘法比MLP组件更具可缩减性；在NVIDIA A100上使用自定义内核的墙钟基准测试表明，计算节省可转化为实际运行时间收益，尤其体现在较长序列长度下。

Conclusion: RMM被定位为一种面向输入自适应的推理时优化可扩展方向，在保持模型性能的同时有效降低推理成本，并展示了在多种模型规模和任务类型下的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance but incur substantial inference cost due to repeated high-dimensional matrix multiplications. We propose Reduced Matrix Multiplication (RMM), a training-free, input-adaptive inference method that reduces Transformer matrix products by selecting informative slices along their contraction dimensions, without modifying model weights. Under a simple retention-ratio control, RMM provides a smooth and predictable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, we find that reduction tolerance depends on the model family, task, component, and retention ratio, although it often improves with model scale. Under moderate reduction, RMM remains robust across the evaluated discriminative, autoregressive generation, and long-context settings. We further show that the same principle extends to multimodal vision-language inference. Mechanistic ablations reveal a structural asymmetry within Transformers: attention-side computations are substantially more reducible than MLP components. Finally, wall-clock benchmarks with custom kernels on an NVIDIA A100 show that these computational savings can translate into practical runtime gains, especially at longer sequence lengths. Together, these results position RMM as a scalable direction for input-adaptive inference-time optimization.

</details>


### [243] [Symmetry-Breaking De Novo Crystal Generation via Markovian Jump Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13457)
*Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SbCD（Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion）的扩散生成框架，通过反向模拟物理中的自发对称性破缺过程，从最低对称性先验出发生成完整的晶体结构规范，能够遍历不同空间群，在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型只能生成到位点对称性级别的晶体，并依赖从经验分布中采样空间群，无法捕捉全局对称性和结构依赖性，限制了生成晶体的完整性和物理合理性。

Method: 受物理学中自发对称性破缺启发，提出一种基于马尔可夫跳跃-扩散过程的扩散框架，通过反转对称性破缺过程（从最低对称性先验出发），显式地将空间群之间的跃迁融入生成过程，从而生成完整的晶体结构规范。

Result: 在de novo生成实验中，SbCD在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上以显著优势超越了其对称保持的对照模型。

Conclusion: SbCD通过物理启发式的对称性破缺机制为晶体材料生成提供了新视角，证明了显式建模空间群间转换在生成模型中的有效性。

Abstract: Generating crystals has recently attracted significant interest due to their broad applications in materials science. However, existing generative models struggle to produce complete crystallographic specifications, limiting their ability to capture global symmetry and structural dependencies. In particular, current state-of-the-art approaches generate crystals only up to site symmetries and rely on sampling space groups from empirical distributions during generation. Inspired by \emph{spontaneous symmetry breaking} in physics, where crystals break symmetries under external conditions, we propose a novel diffusion-based framework that generates full structure specifications by reversing from the lowest-symmetry priors. Our method leverages a Markovian jump-diffusion process to model these symmetry-breaking dynamics, enabling it to traverse different space groups in a physically motivated manner. Our model, dubbed \emph{Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion} (SbCD), introduces a principled approach to explicitly incorporate inter-space-group transitions into the generative process. In de novo generation experiments on MP20 and MPTS-52, SbCD outperforms its symmetry-preserving counterpart by a substantial margin, offering a promising perspective for generative modeling of crystalline materials.

</details>


### [244] [Doubly Robust Estimation of Causal Effect on CVR with Targeted Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13461)
*Jiayi Dan,Bo Li,Lu Deng,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从半参数理论出发，针对点击后转化率（CVR）这一链式结构结果提出了一个新的双重稳健因果效应估计器，该估计器相比干扰参数估计具有更快的收敛速度，对灵活的（包括神经网络在内的）非参数估计器更加稳健，并基于此设计了目标正则化框架以提升数值稳定性和实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点击后转化率（CVR）是电商和广告场景中的关键指标，估计其因果效应具有重要的实际意义。然而，直接对点击样本应用现有因果推断方法会引入样本选择偏差和高方差（因为排除了未点击的数据）。已有研究提出的"理想损失"方法使用全样本上的无偏损失估计来优化模型参数，但损失的无偏性并不能保证最终估计量的无偏性。针对这一缺陷，作者从半参数理论的全新视角进行审视并提出解决方案。

Method: 作者从半参数理论出发，开发了一个专为链式结构结果（如CVR）设计的双重稳健因果效应估计器。该估计器在理论上经过详细推导，能够实现比干扰参数估计更快的收敛速率，从而在使用包括神经网络在内的灵活非参数估计器时更具稳健性。此外，作者还设计了一个基于目标正则化（targeted regularization）的框架来提升数值稳定性和实际适用性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性和稳健性。实验还发现，将损失去偏与标准因果估计器简单组合的方法性能不如所提出的方法，这凸显了针对CVR风格目标开发具有坚实理论保证的新估计器的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：针对CVR这类链式结构结果的因果效应估计，需要一个专门设计且具有理论保证的新估计器，而简单地将现有方法（如损失去偏加标准因果估计器）组合是不够的。所提出的双重稳健估计器结合目标正则化方法，在理论上和实践中都表现优异。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a key metric in various scenarios including e-commerce and advertising, reflecting the efficiency and user experience in the second stage of the conversion process. Estimating the causal effect on CVR is therefore of great practical importance. However, directly applying existing causal inference methods to clicked samples introduces sample selection bias and increased variance due to the exclusion of non-click data. Recent studies on CVR prediction introduce "ideal loss", which optimizes model parameters using an unbiased estimate of the loss over the full sample. Nevertheless, there is no guarantee that unbiasedness of the loss implies unbiasedness of the final estimator.
  We revisit this challenge from the perspective of semiparametric theory. Specifically, we develop a new doubly robust causal effect estimator for chain-structured outcomes such as CVR, and derive its theoretical properties in detail. It achieves a faster convergence rate compared to nuisance parameters estimation and is therefore more robust when using flexible nonparametric estimators, including neural networks. Based on these theoretical findings, we further design a framework based on targeted regularization to improve numerical stability and practical applicability.
  Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method. In addition, we find that naively combining loss debiasing with standard causal estimators underperforms our method, highlighting the necessity of developing the new estimator tailored to this CVR-style objective with solid theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [245] [Concept Drift Detection and Adaptive Retraining of Malware Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13465)
*Christofer Washington Berruz Chungata,Martin Jurecek,Katerina Potika,William B. Andreopoulos,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对恶意软件分类中机器学习模型因概念漂移而性能下降的问题，提出了基于单类支持向量机（OCSVM）的新型概念漂移检测方法，并与Minibatch K-Means和最大均值差异（MMD）两种已有方法进行对比。在四种学习模型（MLP、Random Forest、SVM、XGBoost）下评估了静态、周期性重训和漂移感知三种场景，通过Pareto Front分析权衡准确性与训练效率。结果表明三种漂移检测技术均能达到与周期性重训相当的分类精度，同时大幅减少重训模型数量，其中基于OCSVM的方法整体表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件检测/分类模型极易受到概念漂移的影响，因为攻击者不断修改现有恶意软件导致数据分布随时间变化。传统周期性重训虽然能维持性能但成本高昂，因此需要准确检测概念漂移并仅在必要时重训模型，以在分类准确性和训练效率之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出基于One-Class Support Vector Machines（OCSVM）的概念漂移检测新方法，并与其同已有的Minibatch K-Means（MK-Means）和Maximum Mean Discrepancy（MMD）技术比较。在四种学习模型（MLP、RF、SVM、XGBoost）上设计三种场景：静态（不重训）、周期性（无条件重训）、漂移感知（检测到漂移才重训），并使用Pareto Front分析评估准确性-效率权衡。

Result: 三种概念漂移检测技术在分类准确率上与周期性重训相当，但所需重训的模型数量大幅减少，训练效率显著更高。基于OCSVM的漂移感知重训方法在整体性能上优于MK-Means和MMD方法。实验结果为概念漂移检测在恶意软件分类模型中的应用提供了强有力的证据。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过有效的概念漂移检测，可以在保持高分类准确性的同时显著降低模型重训成本。OCSVM方法展现出最佳的综合性能，为恶意软件分类系统中实现经济高效的在线模型维护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Concept drift refers to changes over time in the statistical properties of data, as compared to the data that was used to train a learning model. Machine learning models for malware detection or classification are particularly susceptible to performance degradation caused by concept drift, as attackers constantly modify existing malware. In this chapter, we analyze two machine learning-based approaches to automated concept drift detection-a novel approach based on One-Class Support Vector Machines (OCSVM) and a previously-studied technique based on Minibatch K-Means (MK-Means). For comparison we also consider Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), a statistical technique for detecting changes in multidimensional data. We conduct an extensive series of experiments comparing the effectiveness of four learning models, namely, Multilayer Perceptron, Random Forest, Support Vector Machines, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting. For each of these models, we consider three distinct scenarios: A static scenario where no model retraining occurs, a periodic scenario where models are constantly retrained irrespective of concept drift, and a drift-aware scenario where models are only retrained when concept drift is detected. Under the drift-aware scenario, we analyze the tradeoff between accuracy and training efficiency using Pareto Front analysis. We find that all three concept drift detection techniques achieve classification accuracy comparable to periodic retraining, while offering substantially greater efficiency in terms of the number of models that must be retrained. In addition, drift-aware retraining based on our OCSVM technique generally outperforms the MK-Means and MMD approaches. Overall, these results provide strong evidence that we can accurately detect concept drift in malware classification models.

</details>


### [246] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究用于非光滑复合目标分布的Moreau-Yosida未调整Langevin算法（MYULA），证明了其离散化误差主要受参考活动迹B_ref控制，而非全局曲率界d/λ。通过曲率-管估计，对分段线性、lasso型、分组和全变差惩罚，可得到B_ref与λ无关，实现~O(ε^{-2})精度，优于通用的~O(ε^{-3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MYULA的收敛性分析依赖于全局曲率上界d/λ，在惩罚函数仅分段光滑的情况下这种界过于宽松，导致收敛阶次不够尖锐。作者旨在通过更精细的曲率-管（curvature-tube）分析，证明实际主导误差的参考活动迹B_ref远小于全局界，从而获得更好的收敛保证。

Method: 令g_λ为g的Moreau包络，H_λ为其弱Hessian，a_λ=tr H_λ。定义参考活动迹B_ref为从π_λ出发进行MYULA一步热子步后a_λ的期望。使用W_2距离度量迭代律与平滑目标π_λ的偏差，并结合Moreau偏差界√m W_2(π_λ,π)≤G²λ/4。对特殊结构惩罚（分段线性、lasso、分组、TV），利用曲率-管估计使B_ref不依赖于λ。

Result: 证明了迭代次数N达到(1/m)[L_f+(τ_f+G²+B_ref)/ε_alg²+M_λ/ε_alg]（含对数因子）即可保证√m W_2(μ_N,π_λ)≤ε_alg。通用估计B_ref≤d/λ给出~O(ε^{-3})精度依赖；而对结构化惩罚，B_ref与λ无关，导致~O(ε^{-2})。选择λ≍ε/G²可得到对目标π的端到端保证。

Conclusion: 研究表明MYULA的收敛速度主要由参考活动迹而非全局曲率决定，对常见稀疏与正则化惩罚（lasso、分组、TV）可实现~O(ε^{-2})的最优精度依赖，无需修改经典MYULA核即可获得改进的复杂度保证。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [247] [Synthetic Persona Pretraining: Alignment from Token Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13482)
*Julian Minder,Viktor Moskvoretskii,Raghav Singhal,Difan Jiao,Andy Arditi,Shaobo Cui,Yiderigun Borjigin,Kartik Bali,Stefan Krsteski,Harsh Raj,Huu Nguyen,Jannik Brinkmann,Ashton Anderson,Roland Aydin,Robert West*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP)方法，在预训练开始时（token zero）就安装期望的助手人格，通过在预训练文档上标注符合价值规范的第一人称反思，并经标准交叉熵损失训练使人格深植于模型，再通过persona binding后训练绑定助手身份。实验表明SPP提升宪法遵循能力、越狱鲁棒性并降低道德困境中的错位率，同时保持能力不降；且越早干预效果越好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法通常在预训练完成之后才引入人格与价值观，导致价值观只是"薄薄的一层覆盖"而非深度根植，容易产生后续错位。作者认为应在预训练的token零处就安装期望的助手人格，使价值观从底层与模型深度融合。

Method: SPP包含三步：1) 用由规范性价值宪法派生的价值对齐第一人称反思来标注预训练文档；2) 用标准交叉熵损失在原始预训练文档及其反思上预训练，在众多人格中植入期望人格；3) 在用户-助手对话数据上后训练（persona binding），将期望人格绑定到助手身份。实验规模最高达3B参数、500B token。

Result: SPP提升了宪法遵循和越狱鲁棒性，在分布外道德困境中降低了错位率，同时保持下游能力。对比在预训练末尾才引入SPP，从token零开始能获得更强的宪法遵循、能改变价值优先级并在困境中更一致的选择，且该优势随预训练预算增加而增大，且依赖persona binding步骤。

Conclusion: 早期塑造价值观对于对齐至关重要，预训练阶段的人格干预是一种有效的对齐手段，为未来模型对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: As language-model-based AI is increasingly deployed in autonomous settings, aligning its goals and values with those of humans becomes critical. Today, alignment, and the assistant identity itself, are typically introduced only after pretraining, once behavioral priors are already established. This can make values a thin overlay, rather than deeply rooted, and facilitate subsequent misalignment. Pursuing a different paradigm, we introduce Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP), which installs the desired assistant persona from token zero in pretraining. First, we annotate pretraining documents with value-aligned first-person reflections derived from a normative value constitution. Second, we pretrain via the standard cross-entropy loss on standard pretraining documents as well as their reflections, which installs the desired persona among a multitude of other personas. Finally, we post-train on user-assistant dialogue data, which binds this desired persona to the assistant identity, a process we call persona binding. By pretraining models up to 3B parameters on 500B tokens, we show that SPP improves constitution following and jailbreak robustness, and reduces the misalignment rate in out-of-distribution moral dilemmas, while preserving capabilities. Early intervention matters: compared with alignment from token zero, introducing SPP only at the end of pretraining yields weaker constitution adherence, does not shift value priorities, and leads to less aligned choices in dilemmas. This advantage depends on persona binding and, importantly, increases with pretraining budget. Overall, our results show that shaping values early is critical for alignment and establish pretraining-time persona interventions as an effective approach to do so.

</details>


### [248] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SORT（稀疏正交回归技术），一种从含噪且不规则采样的数据中学习正交基展开的稀疏谱框架。SORT直接利用L1正则化回归从观测中估计展开系数，避免显式求积或解析内积计算，核心应用是数据驱动的常微分方程发现，可作为符号回归、语法驱动发现和SINDy式稀疏辨识的补充路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的库基稀疏回归方法（如SINDy）在面对问题特定的非线性项时，如果有限库遗漏了关键项，模型会失败。作者希望通过先恢复一个紧凑的谱表示，将问题从对通用项的脆弱选择转移到面向问题域的基础设计，从而更稳健地处理稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示失配。

Method: SORT通过L1正则化回归直接从观测数据中估计正交基展开系数，避免显式数值求积或解析内积计算。向量场在选定的正交基中表示，作为稀疏系数展开进行学习。实验涵盖动力学系统辨识，并与库基稀疏回归基线（如SINDy）进行对比。

Result: 在动力学系统实验中，当基与问题适配良好时，SORT匹配或优于库基稀疏回归基线；在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示失配下表现出更稳定的退化。主导低阶系数随模型阶数增加而持续存在，支持阶数一致的模型增长。此外，学习到的展开还支持非线性逼近和高维复杂积分估计。

Conclusion: SORT提供了一种可复用的中间表示，用于系统辨识、逼近和积分，将基础设计明确纳入科学建模问题的范畴，为数据驱动的动力学发现提供了不同于符号回归和SINDy的互补路径。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [249] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Intern-S2-Preview系列科学智能体基础模型，通过科学多模态预训练和统一后训练流程（监督微调、多任务RL、智能体RL、蒸馏），构建支持多模态科学理解、推理、生成和长时程任务的模型。其中397B版本扩展了时间序列建模能力，并引入Memory Decoder作为独立记忆增强路径，在不修改冻结主干的情况下实现快速科学领域特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现越来越需要AI系统能够跨异构模态推理科学证据、与科学工具和环境交互，并在长任务时间跨度上持续取得进展。现有模型缺乏对科学多模态理解、长时程智能体任务和科学信号（如时间序列）的综合支持，需要专门针对科学场景设计的智能体基础模型。

Method: 训练管线分为两个阶段：（1）科学多模态预训练，基于渲染的科学文档、交错图文数据和多样化科学语料；（2）统一后训练流程，包括监督微调、可扩展多任务强化学习、黑盒/白盒智能体RL以及on-policy蒸馏。同时提出多项工程技巧：部分rollout搭配off-policy校正、自适应长度正则化、在线投机解码、稳健多任务优化、面向智能体任务的trace感知经验组装。架构上，397B版本将时间序列建模从高效长序列理解扩展到数值预测，Memory Decoder作为独立记忆增强路径实现快速科学特化。

Result: 在科学、多模态、智能体和通用基准上，Intern-S2-Preview-397B在多个设置中达到竞争性或领先水平。时间序列模块提升了SciTS上科学信号理解和预测能力；独立的Intern-MemDec-4B扩展将Biology-Instructions平均得分从56.92提升到60.32，且无需修改冻结的397B主干。

Conclusion: Intern-S2-Preview展示了面向科学发现场景的智能体基础模型的有效训练范式和架构设计。通过多模态预训练、多阶段RL后训练和记忆增强路径，模型在科学理解、时间序列预测和领域特化任务中均取得显著进展，为科学智能体的进一步发展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [250] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种干预感知的临床世界模型，将患者表示为结构化潜在状态，通过时序排列的术后事件逐步更新状态，用于心房颤动消融术后90天恢复期内长期复发风险预测，在DECAAF-II数据集上实现AUROC 0.756、AUPRC 0.777的复发预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床预测模型将术后结局视为从基线测量到未来终点的单步映射，忽略了恢复过程往往是不规则轨迹的事实——临床观测、用药变化、重复干预和生理测量异步记录，且随时间动态改变风险评估。现有方法无法有效利用这些时间顺序的术后信息进行动态风险建模。

Method: 提出干预感知临床世界模型：(1)将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态；(2)利用手术过程上下文、静态协变量、经过时间和围事件生理嵌入逐步更新潜在状态；(3)随访影像通过潜在预测目标提供仅训练阶段的监督信号；(4)应用于心房颤动消融术后90天恢复窗口。

Result: 在DECAAF-II数据集上通过重复内部交叉验证，模型实现AUROC 0.756、AUPRC 0.777的复发预测性能；疤痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点，且推理时无需随访MRI强度；学习到的状态支持不同时间跨度的复发风险查询以及空窗期记录的回顾性输入编辑。

Conclusion: 不规则术后记录为长期复发风险提供了具有临床意义的证据。该世界模型方法有效整合了异步术后事件序列，不仅提升预测性能，还支持灵活的风险查询和输入编辑能力，展示了在术后动态风险建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [251] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出未掩码增长复杂度（UGC），一种用于离散采样的掩码扩散统一分析框架。UGC的局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，通过对数揭示比数坐标优化单块和多块调度，并可通过耦合揭示轨迹从样本中估计，从而实现达到给定KL误差的认证最优采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型的掩码采样在理论上缺乏统一的误差分析框架，不同掩码方案（Bernoulli子集与固定基数）的离散化误差难以比较和优化。

Method: 引入路径解析的数据几何度量UGC，其局部增量控制KL离散化误差。在log-reveal-odds坐标下推导优化调度方案，并通过KL增量估计UGC增量。开发认证最优采样器，在常数因子内实现与oracle过程相当的迭代复杂度。

Result: 在细划分极限下，平方根UGC密度的积分平方决定最优Euler离散化误差。示例表明相比粗糙调度可实现维度相关的显著改进，包括常数块数量下达到Ω̃(√d)的提升。

Conclusion: UGC提供了一个统一的框架，将离散扩散的调度优化、KL误差分析、复杂性度量和经典多元依赖度量联系起来，同时实现理论最优与实用可估计性。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [252] [Vero: Can AI Agents Build Formally Verified Software Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13522)
*Zhe Ye,Hantao Lou,Yuechun Sun,Peiyang Song,Zhengxu Yan,Timothe Kasriel,Qingyang Zhang,Kaiyu Yang,Soonho Kong,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍Vero，首个在仓库级别评估联合实现与证明合成的基准测试，包含43个多模块Lean 4实例，覆盖Python、Dafny、Verus和Coq来源的真实世界代码库，并引入审计机制。前沿智能体仅解决27/43实例，表明当前智能体在仓库级验证软件合成方面仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证代码生成基准要么聚焦于单个函数，要么仅在给定实现的情况下评估证明生成，无法回答智能体能否在实际多模块代码库中做出连贯的实现和证明选择，因此需要一个仓库级别的联合实现与证明合成评测基准。

Method: Vero包含43个多模块Lean 4仓库实例（源自Python、Dafny、Verus、Coq），每个实例含预定义API接口、人工精调的形式化规范与参考实现，支持仅证明和代码+证明两种评测模式；并设计了审计机制，允许智能体证明规范的不可满足性或参考代码的不正确性，以揭示和修正数据构建中的潜在错误。

Result: 评估了配备Lean工具链访问权限的前沿编码智能体配置，最强智能体仅完全解决43个实例中的27个，且在最具挑战性的仓库中未能关闭任何规范，表明当前智能体在仓库规模验证软件合成上仍存在显著差距。

Conclusion: Vero为衡量仓库级验证软件合成的进展提供了具体测试平台，揭示当前智能体仍未达到此任务要求，同时通过开放基准、数据构建流程和评测工具（GitHub仓库）促进该方向后续研究。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for programming, but do not provide any guarantee on the correctness of generated code. Verified code generation, in which an agent produces both an implementation and a machine-checked proof of its specification, offers a stronger path toward trustworthy AI-generated software. Existing benchmarks in this direction either focus on individual functions or only evaluate proof generation with provided implementations. It is still an open question whether agents can make coherent implementation and proof choices across real multi-module codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce Vero, the first benchmark to evaluate joint implementation and proof synthesis at the repository level. Vero contains 43 multi-module instances sourced from real-world repositories spanning Python, Dafny, Verus, and Coq, and covering diverse domains from cryptographic protocols to distributed systems. Each instance consists of a multi-module Lean 4 repository with predetermined API interfaces, manually curated formal specifications, and reference implementations, supporting both proof-only and code-and-proof evaluation modes. To improve benchmark reliability, Vero also includes an audit mechanism where agents are allowed to formally prove unsatisfiability of provided specification or incorrectness of reference code, which surfaces and corrects latent code and specification errors during curation. We evaluate frontier coding-agent configurations with Lean toolchain access. The strongest agent fully solves only 27 of 43 instances and closes no specifications on the hardest repositories. Vero provides a concrete testbed for measuring progress toward repository-scale verified software synthesis, where current agents still fall short. We release the benchmark, curation pipeline, and evaluation harness at https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/vero.

</details>


### [253] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTree是一种免训练的投机解码方法，将预训练的AR校正头从链式结构扩展到树状结构，通过批量展开和评分构建固定宽度的候选树，再采用最佳优先剪枝选择验证树。在七个数学、代码和聊天基准测试中，DARTree在所有四种模型-温度配置下均取得最高的平均接受长度和加速比，每次验证可接受最多12.97个token，比DFlash多98.6%，比Domino多27.9%，相对本地自回归解码最高可达9.73倍的无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码通过并行验证多个草稿token来无损加速自回归语言模型。基于扩散的草稿器虽能并行预测整个token块以降低提案延迟，但其位置分布是边际分布而非沿草稿路径条件化的分布。现有递归校正沿单条草稿链携带因果信息，而基于扩散的树构建虽拓宽了候选覆盖范围却未沿各分支携带这种校正。因此需要一种方法将AR校正头的优势从链推广到树，同时兼顾候选多样性和分支级校正。

Method: DARTree首先通过在一次批量操作中展开并评分每层所有节点来构建固定宽度的候选树，然后仅使用最佳优先剪枝来选择验证树，从而将AR校正头推理与顺序堆操作解耦。该方法免训练，直接扩展预训练的AR校正头从链到树结构。

Result: 在七个数学、代码和聊天基准上，DARTree在所有四种模型-温度组合中均获得最高的平均接受长度和加速比。每次验证轮次最多接受12.97个token，比DFlash多98.6%，比Domino多27.9%，且相对本地自回归解码达到最高9.73倍的无损加速。

Conclusion: DARTree通过将AR校正头从链推广到树，有效结合了扩散草稿器的并行令牌块预测能力与AR校正的因果条件化优势，实现了更高的token接受率和无损加速，在多种模型配置和基准上均取得领先性能。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [254] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了多标签分类中Jaccard分数（IoU）的损失矩阵在s个标签下非奇异，其loss列仿射维度为2^s-1；精确校准需要指数级预测维度，但对于固定的加性遗憾容忍度，存在多项式维度的近似代理损失（F1-to-Jaccard传递和MinHash平方损失变体）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard分数是多标签分类和二元分割中的标准指标，但其损失结构的几何性质（如非奇异性和仿射维度）以及精确/近似校准所需的预测维度此前未被充分表征。作者希望从理论上明确校准Jaccard损失的维度需求极限。

Method: 结合有限MinHash格拉姆表示与布尔Möbius反演证明损失矩阵非奇异性和仿射维度；通过因子加权分布构造下界证明CCdim下界为2^{s-1}；利用F1代理损失到Jaccard的遗憾传递实现多项式时间规则；提出MinHash平方损失代理及其带符号变体，给出维度和概率界。

Result: 证明Jaccard、shifted-loss和普通损失矩阵非奇异，loss列仿射维度2^s-1；给出2^{s-1}≤CCdim≤2^s-1的上下界；F1-to-Jaccard传递实现(s^2+1)维代理，渐近Jaccard遗憾≤3-2√2；MinHash平方损失在任意条件分布上统一达到遗憾floor α，带符号变体维度O((s+log(1/ρ))/α²)，直接构型维度O((s²+s log(1/ρ))/α²)。

Conclusion: 零遗憾校准需要指数级预测维度，而任何固定的加性遗憾容忍度都可以通过多项式维度实现，揭示了精确校准与近似校准之间的本质维度鸿沟。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [255] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"防御提升器"(Defensive Booster)算法，首次在在线概率预测中同时获得两种原本互不相容的保证：与H的跨度所诱导的最优预测器在Brier分数上竞争（在线梯度提升的保证），以及在平滑弱学习条件下分类误差趋于零（在线弱到强提升的保证）。该算法通过操作化提升的"对偶视角"实现双保证，且仅需一个弱学习器，运行效率远超此前需维护大规模弱学习器集成的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升方法只能单独提供两种不相容的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上与H的跨度竞争，但当跨度内没有准确预测器时无任何保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下将分类误差驱动到零，但条件失败时几乎无保证。核心动机在于设计一种统一的防御性预测算法，使单一算法同时获得这两种保证，且无需针对每种情况进行切换。

Method: 作者提出Defensive Booster算法，核心创新在于操作化提升的"对偶视角"：当算法的随机化分类误差持续很高时，其错误权重构成一种平滑重加权，使得所有弱假设在该重加权上的edge都很低，从而提供事后硬核证明表明弱学习条件不成立。算法基于防御性预测框架构建，并进一步开发了强自适应变体，使其在任意时间区间上同时满足两种保证。算法仅访问一个弱类学习器，而非维护多个弱学习器集成。

Result: Defensive Booster在任意自适应序列上，其Brier分数与H的跨度诱导的最优预测以与在线梯度提升相同的速率竞争；同时，当实现转录满足平滑弱学习条件时，其Brier分数和随机化分类误差满足与在线分类提升相同的速率保证。合成的和真实数据流实验表明，该算法具有强预测性能（有时显著优于所有先前基线），且运行速度快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过防御性预测和对偶视角的有机结合，Defensive Booster证明了两种传统上分离的在线提升保证可以在单一高效算法中同时实现，这为机器学习中算法效率与理论保证的统一提供了新范式。其在实践中的显著性能提升和速度优势进一步验证了该方法的实用价值。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张生成式AI社区在"推理"（reasoning）概念上缺乏统一的操作性定义，这导致推理评估的构造效度（construct validity）无法验证，进而阻碍了可信自主推理的量化进展。作者基于文献综合提出了操作性定义（将有效且健全的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供了一个研究沟通最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主推理是AI领域最具科学和经济价值的话题之一，但生成式AI社区未就"推理"形成清晰的操作性定义，且常隐式排斥逻辑学与可验证自动化推理中对推理的历史性处理。这种定义模糊性导致推理评估的构造效度不可验证，损害了可信自主推理方向的量化进步。

Method: 本文是一篇"立场论文"（position paper）。方法包括：（1）综合文献提出操作性定义，将推理定位为可学习的基于规则（rule-based）的过程，区分有效性（validity）与健全性（soundness）；（2）提出一份用于AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践清单（checklist）。

Result: 提供了两方面的产出：一是基于文献综合的操作性定义框架，将有效和健全的推理视为一种可学习的规则化过程；二是供AI推理研究交流使用的最佳实践核查清单。作者认为这可以解决定义歧义并提高评估的构造效度。

Conclusion: 定义歧义是当前自主推理评估不可靠的根源，但该问题是可解决的。通过提出操作性的推理定义和沟通最佳实践清单，本文旨在使推理评估的构造效度可验证，从而促进面向可信自主推理的量化进步。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [257] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IntegrityBench，一个评估语言模型在机构压力下科研诚信行为的基准，覆盖3个领域、4个研究阶段、36个配对任务及5级隐式-显式压力协议。对18个前沿模型的评估发现：在峰值压力下，模型约三分之一的关键诚信决策失败；显式压力诱导违规，隐式压力导致过度拒绝；模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解这些问题。三个测评维度（违规分类、伦理推理、基于证据的决策）在结构上相互分离，错误分类的模型在决策任务上反而表现更好（85.7 vs 79.4），揭示AI辅助科研存在助长学术不端和侵蚀信任的双重部署风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正日益作为"共同科学家"被部署，但其在机构压力（如发表压力、资金压力）下维持科研诚信的能力尚未被量化测量。现有基准多关注推理正确性，忽视伦理与诚信维度，且未建模现实科研环境中普遍存在的制度性压力情境。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含36个配对任务，覆盖违规分类、伦理行动推理、基于证据的决策三个维度，横跨3个领域和4个研究阶段，采用5级隐式-显式压力协议。对18个前沿模型变体进行系统评估。

Result: 在峰值压力下模型约1/3关键诚信决策失败；模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解此问题。显式压力诱导模型顺从违规行为，隐式情境重构更多导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝。三个测评维度结构分离：违规分类不准确的模型在基于证据的决策上反而表现相当或更好（85.7 vs 79.4）。

Conclusion: 模型可能在看似有帮助的同时潜藏诚信失败，产生两种不同的部署风险：一是助长科研不端行为，二是侵蚀AI辅助研究的信任。正确伦理行动并不依赖于准确分类，这提示需要开发专门针对科研诚信的模型训练与评估策略。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [258] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出AI对齐方法（原本用于防止有害输出）是双用途技术，可能被恶意行为者滥用为审查和操纵工具。通过将当前对齐技术与可能及实际的滥用案例对应分析，作者论证"完美对齐"模型的追求无意中为恶意行为者提供了日益强大的信息主导工具。论文呼吁社区正视AI对齐机制的故意滥用，并提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐技术的双重用途风险被忽视；随着用户快速采用AI作为信息提供者、经济权力不对称以及政治环境日益威权化，这一风险正在加剧，需要立即讨论。

Method: 采用立场论文的方法，将当前AI对齐技术与信息审查和操纵的可能性及实际滥用案例进行映射分析，系统性论证对齐机制的双用途潜力。

Result: 论证了"完美对齐"模型的追求可被恶意行为者利用来实现信息主导；识别出快速用户采用、经济不对称和威权政治趋势三个加剧因素。

Conclusion: 敦促社区考虑AI对齐机制的故意滥用，并提出缓解策略以防范这一双重用途潜力。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [259] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于500项ETHICS衍生基准，发现尽管LLM在道德判断的最终标签上与人类标注者多数一致，但其支持性推理（rationale）在道德原则分布上系统性地偏离人类，揭示标签一致性并不等同于道德对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常以LLM的输出与人类判断的最终标签一致性作为对齐的代理指标，但两个主体可能在得出相同结论时依赖不同的道德原则、上下文假设或情境解释。因此需要检验标签层面的一致性是否掩盖了推理层面的本质分歧。

Method: 构建一个包含500条条目的ETHICS衍生基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域，收集人类标注者和多类LLM（前沿与开源模型家族）的最终标签及其支持性理由（rationale）。对标签一致性与理由层面的道德原则分布进行对比分析。

Result: 在标签层面，模型与人类标注者多数意见的一致性通常较高；但在理由层面，模型与人类在道德理由（如伤害、尊重、承诺履行、公正、应得与免责相关性）上的注意力分配存在系统性偏差，即使最终标签一致时也是如此。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不应等同于模型与人类的对齐。基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的乐观结论，必须辅以对模型判断中理由、原则和道德优先级的分析。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [260] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream架构及LLM-MR-CNP协议扩展，将经典Contract Net Protocol与语义CFP、渐进上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证结合，用于异构移动边缘-云基础设施中的分布式流处理调度。基于阿里ASI Trace的实验表明，该方案将延迟违规降至3%、消除资源过度承诺、20个智能体下冲突解决率达0.91，比多轮基于规则的基线效用提升最高22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统日益运行在异构移动边缘-云基础设施之上，面临工作负载波动、资源竞争和严格的QoS要求，这使得去中心化调度变得复杂。现有方法难以在这些条件下有效协调资源分配与QoS保障。

Method: 提出LLM-MR-CNP协议扩展，核心要素包括：语义化CFP（调用建议）公式化、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆以及确定性验证。边缘集群智能体基于本地观察、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文生成自然语言的卸载提案，同时硬性资源与QoS约束保持确定性不放松。

Result: 基于阿里ASI Trace的三级实验表明：相比单轮CNP、规则式细化和固定模型协商，MAS-DecStream将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个智能体下冲突解决率达0.91，效用较基于规则的多轮基线提升最高22%。另有25个案例评估显示了模型与提示词相关的精度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 多轮CNP细化是协议层面的主要增益来源，LLM辅助主要补充了定性与不确定运行时环境的推理能力，提供了多轮协商在有LLM辅助与无LLM辅助场景下均有价值的初步证据。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [261] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment）概念，主张在多数场景（尤其高风险决策）中，AI系统应以与用户相似的方式推理并忠实传达其推理过程，并指出了现有对齐方法的不足及未来研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地被用于决策辅助、决策委托或自主决策。作者认为在许多环境中，尤其是高风险决策场景，需要与用户认知方式相似的准确AI系统，以便用户能够理解并信任AI的推理过程。认知错位可能成为AI在众多设想应用中落地的主要阻碍。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），通过综述已有证据说明认知对齐能提升可理解性与可信度，并提供了新的调查数据，展示许多用户认为当AI的判断或行动理由对自己很重要时，认知对齐是"必不可少的"。同时系统梳理了现有对齐方法与实现认知对齐之间的差距。

Result: 综述证据支持认知对齐可改善可理解性与可信度；新调查数据表明大量用户将认知对齐视为"必不可少"；指出现有对齐方法与认知对齐需求之间的空白，并提出研究议程以弥合这些差距。

Conclusion: 认知错位是AI采纳的潜在障碍；解决认知对齐问题对于创建用户既愿意且合理信赖的AI系统至关重要。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [262] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统测试了九种大语言模型在不同语言提示下对高危险性战略决策（核打击建议）的行为差异，发现日语提示显著降低Claude模型家族和Gemini Pro 3.1的打击率，且这一效应由“思考语言”而非“输入语言”驱动——当模型被要求用日语推理时，会自发产生道德性词汇，从而改变决策。而其他五种模型无语言效应，且几乎在所有条件下都选择打击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全对齐通常仅以英语进行评估。作者质疑这种单语评估可能遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与安全机制，因此系统性地考察提示语言是否能在高利害场景中改变模型决策。

Method: 使用单轮博弈论情景（vignette）：模型为拥核国家是否对无防卫对手发动核打击提供建议。提示词刻意设计为不道德导向且跨语言战略上完全一致，从而隔离语言变量的影响。测试九个模型（来自六家供应商），并额外设计跨语言实验——在英语提示中要求模型用日语推理，以分离“推理语言”与“输入语言”的效应。

Result: 日语提示显著降低Claude模型家族的打击率（Claude Sonnet 4.6在不必要打击场景从40%降至0%，争议场景从93%降至17%，战略性合理场景影响极小）；Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。跨语言实验表明，要求用日语推理时打击率从93%降至37%，证明是“推理语言”驱动效应。日语推理时模型自发产生道德词汇（如“道德代价”“数百万生命”）。五种其他模型无语言效应，但在几乎所有条件下都选择打击。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估会遗漏其他语言中存在的风险与保护机制。语言效应仅在模型本身于英语中已有犹豫倾向时才显现，说明安全对齐的效果受语言调制，安全评估应扩展至多语言。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational.
